1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 2027 年聯準會不升息之情境下，全年不鏽鋼、鋁捲板、扣件出貨量都將持續優於2026 年，
不利的風險因子為美國升息，或基礎建設需求中斷，目標價由 55 元(1.4X 26(F)PBR)上調
至 62 元(1.4X 27(F)PBR)，投資評等由【買進】調整至【逢低買進】 ，潛在上漲空間 24.0%。

營 運 分 析

大成鋼為美國鋁捲板、不銹鋼銷售市佔率龍頭 
大成鋼為原先為不銹鋼管製造商，於 2000 年轉型為製造為輔、通路為主之
經營模式，並於全美拓展實體倉庫與據點，發展至今僅剩約 10~15%存貨為
自行製造；主要業務外銷為主占98%，其中美國地區占 92%。主要產品為不
銹鋼(35%)、螺絲螺帽(20%)、鋁捲板(45%)、窗飾及其他產品。 
目前大成鋼為全美不銹鋼產品、工業用鋁捲板、工業用扣件最大通路商： 
1. 旗下大成國際鋼鐵(大國鋼)經 2008 年併購 Brighton Best 後，成為美國唯
一銷售合金鋼、中低碳鋼、不銹鋼三種原料的最大扣件進口通路商，大國
鋼此經營模式長期平均維持 30%以上毛利率。 
2. 2018 年向美國鋁業購置德州工廠，開始製造並銷售鋁捲板，並因中國出
口之工業用鋁捲板對美國廠商造成經營與競爭壓力，大成鋼於2020 年結
合同業對 18個國家之鋁合金板提出反傾銷調查。大成鋼於美國德州擁有
兩條冷軋線，並擴建熱軋線預計於 2025 年底完工，使在美國當地的產能
充足，並視情況調配，目前大成鋼受惠美國稅法政策、在地製造銷售等優
勢為全美市占最大工業用鋁捲板製造通路商。

26Q2不鏽鋼與鋁捲板毛利率受惠特規品項與原物料價差而顯著季增 
大成鋼 26Q2 營收 360.0 億元(QoQ+15.5%/YoY+41.6%)，微幅低於預期
1.9%，主因鋁產品出貨量低於預期，但不鏽鋼與扣件產品出貨量優於預期，
兩項相抵所致；毛利率 30.5%(季增 3.2ppts/年增 6.1ppts)，優於預期
1.9ppts；營業淨利 76.0 億元(QoQ+34.9%/YoY+116.3%)，稅後淨利 47.9 億
元(QoQ+64.2%/YoY+397.7%)，EPS 1.93元，優於預期的 1.80 元。

26Q2 不鏽鋼與鋁捲板出貨概況： 
⚫ 鋁捲板：26Q2出貨量約 7.2萬噸(QoQ+5.8%)，低於預期 8.9%，主因美國
主因 6/18~28號因為聯準會開會後客戶有觀望情緒與出貨排程所致；單季平
均出貨 ASP為每公噸 7,800美元(QoQ+12.2%)；平均毛利率為 28.3%，季
增 4.5個百分點，符合預期。 
⚫ 不鏽鋼：26Q2出貨量 5.0萬噸(QoQ+8.5%)，優於預期 2.9%，受特規不鏽
鋼產品出貨單價較高所致；單季平均出貨 ASP為每公噸 6,877 美元
(QoQ+6.1%)；平均毛利率為 25.8%，季增 2.3個百分點，主因出貨品項占
比調整，符合預期。

單位事業

資料來源：大成鋼、兆豐投顧預估及彙整

美國市場需求與原物料價格持續走揚，26Q2淨利將大幅年增 
近期受中東地緣政治、冶煉廠停復產進度及全球供給預期反覆影響，國際鋁
價波動明顯加劇，LME鋁價自 5月高點回落後震盪整理，美國鋁錠現貨價格
亦同步修正。然而，美國市場在鋼鋁進口關稅維持高檔、當地製造業擴廠及
資料中心建置需求支撐下，進口鋁材的實際到岸成本仍明顯高於國際市場，
使美國本土鋁價相對 LME的溢價持續維持高檔。由最新價格走勢觀察，即使
5月以來 LME鋁價由高點明顯回落，美國鋁錠價格亦未同步收斂至國際價
格，目前兩者價差仍約維持2,000 美元/噸以上，顯示美國鋁價除了受到原料
價格影響外，關稅及區域性供需失衡仍提供相當程度的價格支撐。 
大成鋼主要銷售市場位於美國，在高關稅及當地鋁材需求仍強的環境下，美
國售價與國際鋁價之間的高額價差將延續。即使中東冶煉廠較原先預期提前
恢復生產，可能舒緩全球鋁供應緊張，但考量中東至美國航線運力仍有限、
海運成本處於相對高檔，加上美國進口關稅負擔，因此對美國本土鋁價的壓
力相對可控。

聯發科 (2454 TT)  Buy

輝達認購聯發科 ECB，將帶動聯發科短期評價，投資建議
給 Buy。  
投資建議 ：輝達 (NVDA US) 認購聯發科 ECB ，有助於短期評價
與長期營運，投資建議給 Buy 。  
輝達認購聯發科 ECB：輝達宣布斥資 35 億美元，認購聯發科的
ECB ，其餘 ECB 認購公司包含 Alphabet(GOOG US) 。  
聯發科將資金用於後續 AI 商機：聯發科此次募資資金將用於多
項用途，除了強化全球供應鏈外，也將持續加碼晶片設計、
ASIC 、互連 IP 以及先進封裝能力，藉此支援後續更大型的 AI
專案。  
雙方將有更多合作： 未來加強合作領域包含以下三點。一、 AI
基礎建設，聯發科將會基於 NVLink Fusion 生態系，協助客戶
開發基礎建設，並整合進 NVIDIA 的機櫃及 AI 工廠。二、邊緣
AI 運算，雙方會開發更多 RTX Spark 與 DGX Spark 的電腦晶
片。三、車用，雙方將持續開發具備 AI 功能的軟體定義汽車平
台。  
輝達投資聯發科，有助於聯發科 XPU 商機： 聯發科已與部分潛
在客戶展開接洽， NVIDIA 直接投資聯發科，可讓客戶更願意採
用聯發科 XPU 搭配 NVLink Fusion 的整體方案，也可讓雙方擴
大供應鏈與產品版圖。  
群益預估聯發科 2026 年 \2027 年稅後 EPS 60.57 元 \80.92 元：

元， YoY-7.76%\+33.61% ；稅後 EPS 60.57 元 \80.92 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
產品組合：  
2Q26 產品組合：手機晶片業務 41% 、智慧裝置平台業務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手機業務全年展望保守：  
2Q26 手機晶片業務營收 QoQ-14%\YoY-20% ，營收占比 41% ，衰退原因是
手機的 BOM 表的成本上漲，聯發科預計 2026 年全球手機出貨量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。 3Q26 手機業務展望季持平或季減中個位數百分
比，旗艦級 SoC 的量產可以抵消大部分其他手機業務的衰退。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
智慧邊緣平台業務展望正向：  
2Q26 智慧邊緣平台業務營收 QoQ+19\YoY+26% ，營收占比 53%。成長動能
包含在運算、車用的市佔率的提升，以及 TV SoC 的成長。多項連接、車用
項目走向量產，聯發科預計此業務將季增中高個位數營收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營收占比約 6%：  
2Q26 PMIC 業務營收 QoQ+11%\YoY+6% ，營收占比 6%。成長動能包含資
料中心的市占率提升，聯發科預計此業務將季持平。

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業務展望更樂觀：  
聯發科第一個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開始量產，預計 2026 全年資料中
心將貢獻超過 20 億美元，並且在 2027 年倍數成長，聯發科預計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年的市場規模將達到 800 億美元，聯發科目標市佔率從 10~15% ，上修
至 15~20% 。第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 2028 年初量產，聯發科有信心在
2028 年的市占率將提升。  
聯發科認為每代的 ASIC 的毛利率相當，並不會更高， ASIC 的毛利率低於
目前公司平均，但新世代的 ASIC 對營收的貢獻貴更大。  
其他補充：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的研發順利，採用 CPO 的解決方案。 448G SerDes 之
後，聯發科會在台積電 (2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平台上，繼續發展 CPO 的解決
方案。聯發科還有提供 3.5D 的平台，該平台提供 IP、封裝與設計流程，適
用於大型資料中心的設計。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。聯發科目前還在投入新技術，絕對費用金額還會
再增加，但是由於營收規模上升，費用率會下降。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 2

Asia Pacific Semiconductors and Electronic Components &
Equipment
27 August 2026 Citi Research
2
•Custom ASIC Competition –   NVIDIA framed the ASIC competition around the economics and flexibility of the entire
“AI factory.” NVIDIA’s competitive advantage increasingly comes from integrating GPU compute, CPU, networking,
rack-scale architecture and software into one platform that can support multiple models and workloads across
different clouds. This changes the competitive discussion from whether a custom ASIC can outperform a GPU on a

specific task to whether hyperscalers can reproduce the same flexibility, software ecosystem and infrastructure
efficiency at scale. In our view, custom ASICs remain a credible competitive threat, particularly for stable and highly
optimized inference workloads, but NVIDIA's broader platform approach can defend its position even as the
accelerator market becomes. We would expect NVLink fusion more ready with AWS first in Trainium 4 generation, we

see Alchip would be the beneficiary as the major partner. We also see that Mediatek's early engagement with Nvidia
could be helpful for the company to generate more opportunity for second customer in ASIC in the longer term.
Companies Mentioned:

Precision (2317.TW; NT$252.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | Mediatek (2454.TW; NT$3865.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | NVIDIA Corp

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

2Q26 beat: Inventec’s 2Q26 revenue was 10% / 14% higher than our estimate / 
Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server shipments ramp up and PC 
demand pull-in. GM of 4.2% was lower than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, 
reflecting the rising component cost weighing on the company’s profitability, such as 
PCB, memory, CPU, etc. 2Q26 opex ratio was better than our estimate and Bloomberg 
consensus, reflecting the company’s improving operational efficiency as revenue scales 
up, along with the higher than expected non-op income, leading 2Q26 net income 27% 
higher than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Inventec’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and slightly cut 
our 2026-28E net income by 5% / 6% / 1%, mainly on lower GM estimates. Our 
2026-28E revenues are revised up to reflect the company’s strong AI server business 
growth, riding on the growing AI infrastructure trend globally and enjoying a product 
mix upgrade towards AI servers with stronger performance and higher ASP . We cut our 
2026-28E GMs to reflect the 2Q26 GM miss and the impact of rising component costs. 
2026-28E opex ratios are revised down, mainly on higher operational efficiency as AI 
server shipments grow. We raise our interest expenses in 2026-28E to reflect Inventec’s 
debt level increase, supporting the company’s global capacity expansion and rising 
working capital level.

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP , and our target P/E is 
updated to 14.6x 2027E P/E (vs. 13.6x previously), mainly on higher earnings growth in 
2027-28E, still derived from PC/server peers’ correlation between forward year trading 
P/E and earnings growth. With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$56.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.6x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

•MediaTek becomes a much more credible AI ASIC contender . MediaTek 
already expects its first major AI accelerator ASIC to enter production in 
4Q26, has guided to >$2bn of data-center revenue in 2026, estimates a 
roughly US$80bn 2027 addressable/serviceable market, and has raised its 
target share to 15–20%.   The NVIDIA relationship therefore potentially 
expands MediaTek’s opportunity beyond its existing hyperscaler programs: 
NVLink Fusion can become a customer-acquisition platform for future 
second customer's ASIC wins as NVLink Fusion changes the competitive 
proposition: instead of MediaTek selling customers only an independently 
designed ASIC, it can potentially offer a custom XPU already engineered to 
coexist with NVIDIA GPUs, CPUs, networking and rack-scale NVLink 
infrastructure. According to NVIDIA, the cooperation will be deepening 
including : NVIDIA NVLink Fusion chiplet, connecting XPUs to the NVIDIA 
NVLink scale-up fabric using NVIDIA photonics or electrical 
interconnects;  NVIDIA NVLink-C2C, providing high-bandwidth, energy-
efficient connectivity between XPUs, NVIDIA Rosa CPUs and other 
compatible processors. NVIDIA NVHBM, integrating customized memory 
capabilities to increase bandwidth and energy efficiency while dedicating 
more silicon area to compute.
•MediaTek is evolving from a smartphone SoC company into an AI 
compute-platform . MediaTek and NVIDIA announced the GB10 platform 
last year, where MediaTek contributed Arm CPU/SoC and power-efficiency 
expertise to the Grace Blackwell-based DGX Spark platform.   That 
relationship has already expanded into RTX Spark for Windows AI PCs, and 
today’s announcement explicitly says the two companies intend to

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
2
collaborate across multiple generations of both RTX Spark and DGX Spark products.   MediaTek therefore gains three
separate AI growth vectors: (1) hyperscaler custom AI ASICs, (2) NVIDIA-based AI PCs/local AI systems, and (3)
automotive/physical AI. Strategically, this is important because MediaTek can reuse the same core capabilities—
leading-edge CPU/SoC design, SerDes/interconnect, advanced packaging, connectivity and power optimization—

across all three markets. The result should be a progressively higher mix of large, advanced-node chips with much
greater dollar content than traditional smartphone SoCs, reducing MediaTek’s dependence on handset cycles.
•We view today’s announcement as structurally positive for MediaTek. The US$3.5bn investment validates the
partnership financially, it potentially moves MediaTek from being a supplier that happens to have a few large AI ASIC
wins toward becoming one of the principal custom-silicon design platforms inside the broader NVIDIA AI-factory

ecosystem. That could materially increase both the number and longevity of MediaTek’s AI ASIC opportunities in the
longer term.

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 082: 260902_daiwa_NYPCB

Source PDF: all report pdf/260902_daiwa_NYPCB.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-02

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
SEMICON Taiwan 2026
Takeaways
Key takeaways from CEO fireside chat, which included Hon Hai (HH) Chairman
Young Liu and Unimicron (UMTC) Chairman Shan-Chieh Chien:
• HH sees AI demand as  unprecedented in speed and scale, pressuring the
supply chain to expand capacity while raising capital intensity.
• Management views diversification as structural, accelerated by geopolitics
and countries' push for local production. We have written about this here,

here, and here.
• HH's strategy is "Made with Taiwan", exporting Taiwan's manufacturing IP,
system-integration know-how and execution speed to end markets via local
partnerships and production.
• UMTC said AI lifts ABF substrate demand and complexity (large sizes, higher
layer counts, more advanced designs), widening supply constraints. This
aligns with our view, which we have written here, here, and here.
• UMTC said it has held more than 10 meetings in the past 18 months with

suppliers and customers to align demand forecast and capacity, by actively
preparing capacity, preparing new facilities, securing land.
• Supply of upstream key materials, such as ABR and T-glass, remains tight.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 088: 260903_daiwa_ Laiyi

Source PDF: all report pdf/260903_daiwa_ Laiyi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-03

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We received an update from Lai Yih’s management after its 
upbeat 2Q26 results (see memo, 25 August). We are more confident in its 
revenue and operating profit returning to growth YoY in 2H26E and 2027E 
on new model launches from its key clients, a new client win and better 
seasonality approaching in 4Q26E, and thus, upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) 
from Hold (3).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results key highlight: better-than-expected 
client and product mix. In 2Q26, we noticed that Lai Yih’s revenue and 
gross margin beat expectations on more favourable client and product mix. 
Its operating profit of TWD1.203bn (+566.6% QoQ and +8.7% YoY) beat 
our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by a respective 26.6% and 
41.1%. Lai Yih’s client mix for 2Q26 included Adidas (53.4%; -2.5% YoY), 
Hoka (36%; +9.0% YoY, record-high revenue mix level), Converse (7.8%; -
41.5% YoY) and New Balance (2.8%; -19.6% YoY).

Brighter 2H26E and 2027E outlook with rising new model launches 
from existing clients; we assume one new client in 4Q26. We believe 
3Q26E will be off-peak season for Lai Yih, but expect its revenue 
momentum to pick up QoQ in 4Q26E, along with gross margin expansion 
QoQ. Given the company’s track record with Adidas and Hoka, 
management noted it is in discussion with several potential new clients; we 
assume one new client to onboard in 4Q26. Lai Yih aims for 2027 
shipments to return to 2025 levels. Despite continuing inflation, the 
company targets to gain more high-end shoes with higher ASP to drive its 
overall ASP and gross margin expansion. Besides, Hoka will start to initiate 
new products in 2H26, which we believe will be positive to Lai Yih’s gross 
margin. Our 2026-27E EPS remain almost unchanged, and we introduce 
our 2028 forecasts, calling for EPS growth of 10% YoY.

What we recommend: We upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) from Hold (3) and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD230 (from TWD160) mainly on improving 
fundamentals expected from 2H26E. Due to a recovering earnings 
trajectory, we now use a target PER of 18x (from 15x), vs. its past-3-year 
range of 12-33x (average PER of 20x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS. 
We forecast an earnings CAGR of 18.5% over 2026-28 (previously -1.6% 
over 2025-27E). Downside risks: weaker-than-expected end demand; 
lower-than-expected market share gain; inflation.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 15-22% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative on revenue and gross 
margin. However, we believe we are more positive on Lai Yih’s client win 
capability riding on the product mix optimisation trend.

3 September 2026

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Lai Yih is a footwear manufacturer that collaborates with Adidas (56.4% of total 
revenue in 2025), Hoka (28.5%), Converse (12.4%) and Uniqlo. Its expertise lies in the production of 
high-quality vulcanised shoes, athletic footwear, cold pressed shoes, and specialised functional 
footwear. Lai Yih Group is headquartered in Taichung (Taiwan) and its production plants and 
development centres for clients are mainly based in Vietnam (91% of total shipment in 2025), Myanmar 
(7%) and Indonesia (1%).

Lai Yih Footwear (6890 TT): 3 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 3 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Lai Yih is not the same individual. We note that the CEO, Mr. Lin,
has 35 years of experience in this industry, which is a positive sign from an executive
perspective.
Capital management 1 Lai Yih's dividend payout ratio was 44-70% over 2022-25, which we see as rational due to its
capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during
2022-25, and we note no suspicious activity.
G Supply chain
management 2
In supply chain management, the company emphasises product quality and safety. Due to its
focus on supply chain transparency, it has designated sales specialists to provide suppliers with
prompt responses to issues.
E Materials sourcing &
efficiency 2
For raw materials and energy usage, the company prioritises environmental sustainability. Its
production processes adhere to safety certifications and standardized procedures, while it strives
to improve energy efficiency and reduce pollution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Update
AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 4
Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Stock prices in our  upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews
negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730

peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings based on 60% share/125k switches/9mn
FAUs, US$175 FAU ASP , US$1.58bn TAM).

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

壹. 欣興公告重訊表示妨害農工商罪中產地標示有爭議罪嫌 
欣興(3037)重訊表示檢調單位告知主要係妨害農工商罪中產地標示有爭議，配合調查對象為
1位總經理、1位副總及相關單位同仁。

貳. 針對商品「原產國或品質」進行虛偽標示及涉及進行販賣 
就欣興搜索案由來看，欣興公告涉及「妨害農工商罪」，依我國刑法第十九章規定，相關罪
名涵蓋第 251 至 255 條，其中與電子製造業較具關聯者主要為第 255 條。依刑法第 255 條
規定，若意圖欺騙他人，就商品「原產國或品質」進行虛偽標記或其他表示，或明知為前述
商品仍進行販賣、陳列及自外國輸入，均可能涉及相關刑責。

欣興電子為台灣 ABF載板、PCB(HDI)與多層板產製廠商

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理 
參. 對於欣興營運層面實質影響有限 
觀察欣興此舉是公司在合江廠產製之多層板，運送回台灣換貼產地標示，產品與場認定之
ABF 載板無關。目前的罪名洗產地可能面臨罰款、課徵懲罰性關稅，最嚴重是出口許可管制
等，而非涉及市場傳言之內線交易、做假帳等重大缺失。再者，後續要觀察此案涉及之多層
板，是否後續的 12 道在台灣廠區進行的加工製程，可以提升附加價值超過 35%為原則而論
定。所謂的洗產地，意指將某國(如中國)生產的商品或原料，運至第三地(如台灣、越南、墨
西哥等)進行極為簡單的加工、改包裝或偽造產地證明，藉此規避進口國的高額關稅 (如反傾

肆. 2026年欣興營運展望仍維持相對樂觀 
欣興於 2026年 7月份法說會中指出，26 年資本支出從 254 億元增至 340 億元(欣興再度上
修資本支出至 537 億元，以及新增長交期設備支出至 376 億元)，主因為因應各項 AI 新產
品的釋出，欣興在光復廠(光復 1、2 廠)的 ABF 載板客戶拉貨進程加速，光復 1 廠客戶拉
貨進度優於預期呈現滿載，籌備光復2 廠的建廠進度，後續2026 年光復 2 廠學習曲線可望
加速滿足 ASIC客戶訂單新增需求；此外，欣興楊梅2 廠 ABF 載板、進而2027 年楊梅 3 廠
產能進程加速進行。根據種種跡象指出，欣興於 2026 年對於 ABF 載板(ABF 佔欣興營收超
過 50%)市況轉趨樂觀，且2H26記憶體需求帶動之下 BT載板市況也轉趨樂觀。在PCB/HDI
方面，欣興可供貨高於 60~80L 之高層數 HDI)、mSAP、HDI 新製程與良率提升對欣興獲
利提升有所挹注，因此，評估此案，對於欣興長期營運基本面與財務概況影響有限。

伍. 國內相關廠商可望承接轉單與否 
觀察目前 ABF 載板、PCB 上中下游產業鏈，均呈現供不應求之情況，涵蓋 AI 伺服器用之
HVLP4 高階銅箔、T-Glass 載板用玻纖與 Low Dk PCB 板用玻纖、PCB 產製設備與鑽孔/
鑽針耗材、銅箔基板(M7~M8)主流規格受惠於 AI伺服器需求帶動呈現價格高漲；以及延伸到
PCB/HDI、高階厚板製程供需也呈現緊俏、ABF載板受惠於承載晶片數量與規格升級朝向高
階 ABF載板需求日增、甚而BT載板需求也受惠於消費性電子與(HBM)記憶體應用擴增後對
於傳統記憶體 SSD Controller需求持續提升所致。涵蓋各個CCL/PCB/ABF/BT板材產製環
節均呈現供不應求，以致於，即使欣興因此案會有轉單效應，其他友廠暫時無力承接(無足夠
產能承接)，再者市場上高階 ABF載板與 HDI高層板之供貨廠商為數不多，轉單效應有限。

投資建議:觀察近日籌碼沉澱後，後續基本面的營運轉機 
觀察此次洗產地案件，市場氣氛上不利，容易造成市場對欣興本身公司治理、客戶關係及後
續處分的疑慮，即使尚未證實公司有重大違法行為，股價仍可能出現回檔修正之疑慮。然而，
現階段不宜直接推論為對於欣興營收、毛利率、ABF載板或 AI PCB/HDI板業務受到重大影
響。因此，宏遠投顧研究部，展望2026 年，預估欣興年營收為1777.06 億元，+35.40%YoY，
毛利率為 24.64%，稅後淨利為 357.51 億元，稅後 EPS+22.96 元；展望 2027 年，預估欣
興年營收 2519.95 億元，+41.80%YoY，毛利率為 30.58%，稅後淨利為 513.71 億元，稅
後 EPS+33.68元，對於欣興(3037)維持正向看法。

力旺(3529 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子上游 
投 資 速 報

先進製程、 Security IP 與 Logic Flash   
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
3nm 陸續量產、 PUF 加速滲透 AI 資 料 中 心 ， 並 升 級 至 Security 
Sub system ； 1T NeoFlash 拓展 Standalone Flash ，持續擴大市場空
間 。 預估力旺 (3529 T T)  2026 年 EPS 34.11 元， 2027 年 動 能 聚 焦 3nm
與 PUF 權 利 金 放 量 ， Caliptra 提升 IP 價值 ， EPS 44.02 元 ， 建 議 給 予 本
益比 80 倍 ， 設 定 目 標 價 3520 元（ 80X ＊ 2027(F) ），評等「買進」。  
留意 Synopsys 等 PUF IP 業者競爭，以及大型晶片廠自研 Hardware 
Root of Trust 方 案 ， 可 能 影 響 第 三 方 PUF IP 滲 透 率 。

◼ 重點摘要  
AI 伺 服 器 帶 動 記 憶 體 分 層 重 組 、 高 速 互 連 升 級 與 安 全 架 構 強 化  
市場預期未來 3 ～ 5 年 AI 伺服器與加速器維持約 20% CAGR ，雲端業者
CapEx 亦 維 持 雙 位 數 成 長 ， 帶 動 2024 ～ 20 27 年 HBM （高頻寬記憶體）
位元需求年增 30 ～ 40% ，並推升高速 SRAM （ 靜 態 隨 機 存 取 記 憶 體 ） 、
大容量 DRAM （ 動 態 隨 機 存 取 記 憶 體 ） 、 CXL （ 運 算 快 速 連 結 ） 記 憶 體 池
化及 SSD Controller （固態硬碟控制晶片） 、 BMC （基板管理控制器） 需
求。 此外， NIS T （ 美 國 國 家 標 準 與 技 術 研 究 院 ） 已 確 立 首 批 PQC （後量
子密碼學）標準，市場預期 20 27 年 前 後 啟 動 後 量 子 安 全 部 署 ， 提 升 RoT
（信任根）與 PUF （物理 不可複製功能）安全 IP 的重要性。

2

公 司 簡 介 — 邏 輯 製 程 嵌 入 式 非 揮 發 性 記 憶 體 技 術 市 佔 全 球 第 一  
力旺電子專注於邏輯製程嵌入式非揮發性記憶體（ Embedded NVM ）矽
智 財 開 發 ， 為 全 球 首 創 邏 輯 製 程 可 程 式 NVM 矽 智 財 廠 ， 相 關 技 術 市 占
全 球 第 一 。 公 司 以 IP 技 術 授 權 與 設 計 服 務 為 主 要 商 業 模 式 ， 透 過 授 權
金 、 設 計 費 及 量 產 權 利 金 獲 利 。 核 心 技 術 涵 蓋 NeoBit 、 NeoFu se 等
OTP ／ MTP ，以及可重複讀寫的 NeoFlash 、 NeoE E ， 提 供 不 同 密 度 、 抹
寫次數及操作電壓規格，目前營收主要來自 NeoFuse 與 NeoBit 。終端
應用廣泛， 12 吋晶圓涵蓋 TDDI 、 OLED 、 DRAM 、 ISP 、 DT V、 STB 及
Wi -Fi 6 、 Bluetooth 、 Switch 、 T WS 等 網 通 產 品 ； 8 吋則以 LCD 驅動
IC 、電源管理 IC 、 MCU 、 Sensor 、 指 紋 辨 識 及 車 用 產 品 為 主 。

受 惠 先 進 製 程 較 高 ASP 、新 品 量產  
力旺 2Q2026 營收 11 億元， QoQ +0.2% ， YoY +17.1% ， 營益率
60.7% ， QoQ +0.2ppts ， YoY +2.5ppts ， EPS 7.76 元。 營收年增主要
受授權金帶動，其中 PUF（ AI ／ Data Center ）、 NeoFuse 成長強勁 。預
估 力旺 3Q2026 營收 12 億 元 ， QoQ +9.1% ， YoY +25.7% ， 營 益 率
61.9% ， QoQ +1.2ppts ， YoY + 4.4ppts ， EPS 8.5 元。 看好授權金維持
強 勁 成 長 動 能 ， 權 利 金 則 受 惠 先 進 製 程 較 高 ASP 、 新 產 品 陸 續 量 產 、
PUF 放量及 MTP 占 比 提 升 ， 成 長 可 望 進 一 步 加 速 。

圖一、邏輯製程 非 揮 發 性 記 憶 體

資 料 來 源 ： 公 司 ， 康 和 投 顧 整 理 。

先 進 製 程 與 安 全 IP 放量  
隨先進製程 Tape -out 增 加 ， 授權金與權利金 ASP 同步提升， 3nm 產品
陸續量產，預期 2027 年 貢 獻 更 明 顯 。 PUF -Based Security IP 客戶晶片
加 速 量 產 ， 應 用 由 AI ／ AGI CPU 、 BMC 拓 展至 AI Data Center SSD 、
CXL 、 光 通 訊 及 高 速 介 面 晶 片 ， 並 延 伸 至 AI Accelerator 與 AI ASIC 。隨
Caliptra 逐步普及，公司由單一 Security IP 朝整合 NeoPUF 、 NeoFuse
及 TRNG 的 Sec urity Subsystem 發展 。

AI 硬 體 安 全 規 範 加 速 普 及  
Caliptra 由 Microsof t 、 AMD 、 Google 及 NVIDIA 等大廠自 2022 年共
同 推 動 ， 逐 步 成 為 AI ／ Data Center 晶片 Hardware Root of Trust 的重
要 開 放 式 安 全 架 構 ； 力 旺 指出， NVIDIA Vera Rubin 為首個導入
Caliptra 1.0 的 運 算 世 代 ， 有 望 帶 動 相 關 Security IP 需 求 ， 惟 目 前
NVIDIA 公 開 產 品 資 料 尚 未 直 接 揭 露 Caliptra 採 用 細 節 。

與 CSP 與 AI 晶片 客 戶 合 作  
核心技術 NeoBit 、 NeoFlash 及 NeoEE 具低成本、高效能、高良率與良
好跨製程整合能力。隨成熟製程價格回穩、製程節點持續推進及 chiplet
架構逐步普及， IP 導入 彈性提升，有助公司維持穩健成長動能。 PUF 技
術已整合進 VeraRubin 架構，並與 CSP 及 多 家 伺 服 器 與 AI 晶 片 客 戶 合
作， 布局涵蓋 HBM 、 SRAMRepair 、 CXL 記憶體池、 SSDController 與
BMC 等資料中心相關應用場景。

Caliptra Security Subsystem 提升單案價值

專 屬 祕 密 ， 搭 配 NeoFuse 提供 Protected Stor age ， 並 以 Physical 
Noise Source ／ TRNG 提供 Trusted Entropy ，進一步整合為
Caliptra -compatib le Security Subsystem ，支援 Root of Tr ust 、
Secure Boot 及 Key Protection 等功能。 公司 Security IP 已 成 功 整 合 主
要 CPU 廠 最 新 一 代 AI ／ AGI CPU 平 台 ， 並 由 BMC 拓展至 AI Data 
Center SSD 、 CXL 、 光 通 訊 及 高 速 介 面 晶 片 。
